one or more electrons and protons with electrode or electrolyte ·nuclei, (2) sequential fusion react­
ions between protons and (3) multibody fission reactions. During the first of ENTs, the capture of
 electrons or protons occurs in the itonic electrode or electrolyte clusters so that the newly
transmuted elements distribute near the original one on the periodic table. Since the capture takes
 place sequentially, the transmutation rate is high. The second of ENTs occurs in the itonic hy­
drogen cluster so that the products distribute among light elements such as Al, Si, S and Cl. The
third of ENTs is multibody fission reactions which are resulted by the decay of compound heavy
nuclei, and will be discussed in Sec. 4.e. Furthermore electro-nuclear regeneration(ENG) which
will be described in Sec. 4.f could be an alternative ENT in a broad sense.
4.d ENC Forming Tiny Black and White Holes
         As the itonic bonding becomes more intense in a high current density, the ENR process
further proceeds. Then the itonic cluster wholy shrinks and the most remarkable ENR, ENC,
takes place in the itonic cluster locally or totally. When the current density is sufficiently high,
the whole cluster collapses. Here many hydrogen or heavy element nuclei which are compressed
to fuse each other collapse and eventually form a tiny black hole. Since the collapse is induced
not by the gravitational force but by the electromagnetic force with the itonic electrons, no ex­
tremely huge mass is required for the collapse. The white hole is expected to be connected with
                                                                                      33
the black hole through a worm hole having an extremely thin diameter, about 10· cm. The
formation of the tiny black hole is difficult to prove, but the tiny white hole is easily observed.



                                               - 18 -

                                                -67-
Since perfectly broken materials could be emitted from the white hole, they should cause a lot of
curious phenomena which we have never seen so far.
        In the previous experiments of electrolysis with a thin nickel foil, the formation of tiny
black and white holes was already observed on nuclear emulsions(4, 13, 29). We obtained an
especially remarkable trace which showed that an explosion caused by the black hole and an
eruption of broken materials from the white hole simultaneously took place, as shown in Fig. 7 in
 Ref. 29. This phenomenon could be explained by the property of the extremely thin diameter of
 the worm hole. When too much materials collapse to form the black hole, all of them could not
penetrate through the worm hole instantaneously so that some potion of materials transformed to
the black hole could explode. Then the conventional elements such as C and O are regenerated
from the collapsed materials: the thin C tubes could be generated from the eruption of the white
hole and the thin C films from the explosion of the black hole, respectively.
4.e. Electr�Nuclear Multiple Fission Reaction(EMF)
         It is amazing but very important that the conventional elements such as C and O were
generated during ENC of the Pb clusters. During ENC, there should be two different branches to
generate other elements by depending on the compression strength: electr�nuclear multiple
fission reaction(EMF) and electr�nuclear regeneration(ENG) through tiny black and white
holes. ENG which could take place in the highest compression is one kind of nuclear transmuta­
tion in a broad sense but should be strictly distinguished from ENT mentioned in Sec. 4.c, be­
cause nuclei are completely destroyed during ENG so that super elementary particles should be
introduced, as will be discussed in the following sections.
         Just before ENC takes place in an itonic cluster, many nuclei could fuse each other to
form a large compound nucleus. If the compression is enough, ENC could be induced. When the
compression is not so strong, the compound nucleus could not lead to ENC but be scattered as
multiple fission fragments. This reaction could be called "Electr�Nuclear Multiple Fission
Reaction(EMF)." While the compression continues, the exciting energy of the compound nucleus
 which is created by multiple fusion reactions could be transferred to the vibrational motion of



                                              - 19 -


                                               -68-
the itonic electrons. Then the compound nulceus could be reduced to a minimum energy state, in
which the constituents of the compound nucleus could be divided into several intranuclear clus­
ters. The intranuclear clusters could be light nuclei such as He, C, 0 and so on. When the nuclear
 energy is beyond the binding one caused by the itonic electrons, the compound nuclei could be
scattered as mupltiple fission fragments. EMFs were already observed as star like traces on nu­
clear emulsions in the previous experiment of cold fusion(30).
        Recently it was reported that a Cu isotope was generated during the discharges with a
natural thorium metal(31 ). Here a compound nucleus which was generated from a Th nucleus by
capturing a number of protons might be strongly compressed and divided into a few pieces of
intranuclear clusters. During the compression, the minimum energy state could be formed such
that it could be divided into tetrahedrons of same nuclei. In the case of   232   Th, the compound nu­
cleus resulted by capturing 28 protons could be divided into four ., Cu isotopes(32). No energetic
 radiations also could be followed.
         Here we have to comment: no EMFs could cause the generation of the thin C tubes and
films which were mentioned in the preceding chapter. We had important information about the
distribution of the generated elements. In the C tubes, light elements such as C and O were found
 in a peripheral region, while heavy elements such as Fe near at the center of the C tubes, res­
pectively. On the other hand, elements of C, 0 and heavy ones including up to Fe uniformly dis­
tributed in the C films. Such curious distribution of the elements as well as the strange shapes of
the C tubes could not be explained by EMFs at all. More drastically different mechanisms, ENG,
should be proposed.
4.f. Elecrto-Nuclear Regeneration(ENG)
         During ENC with a high compression, the compound nucleus could transform to an ex­
tremely tiny black hole. The black hole should be so tiny that instantaneously evaporate. When
the compression is further strong, the black hole could lead to a white hole through an extremely
thin worm hole. Here completely broken materials could be erupted from the white hole into the
space. When too much materials are compressed at the same time, they could not penetrate the



                                              - 20 -

                                               -69-
worm hole so that the explosion of the black hole and the eruption of the white hole could take
place simultaneously. They could generate the thin C films and tubes. Such simultaneous reaction
 was already observed on a nuclear emulsion in a previous cold fusion experiment(29). The ele­
ments which were observed in the thin films and tubes are conventional ones: mainly C and 0,
and a small amount of Si, Fe and so on. The EDX spectra indicated precise patterns of X-rays for
 all the elements. It is very amazing that the materials which were broken by ENC were regene­
rated as the conventional elements, of course, different ones from that before ENC. This fact
suggested that we have to enter a world with an extremely small dimension. The regeneration of
the elements should be explained by some more fundamental particles which could hardly sustain
 their shapes during passing through the worm hole with the diameter of 10 ·" cm.
4.g. Super Elementary Particles: Misson and Cellron
         In order to comprehensively understand all of the ENR phenomena, The Nattoh Model
has to be expanded to deal with the extremely small domain, IO·" cm. Here we introduce a new
hypothetical theory for the subnuclear domain. The theory could be based on an important hint
which was already obtained. It is ENG which should require new components of at least two
different kinds, because only one component should fail to explain the regeneration of the des­
troyed materials. Now we introduce two super elementary particles, called "misson" and "cell­
ron." Misson is a tiny particle which is distributed in cellron and takes a role of particle like be­
haviors for the conventional particles such as electrons and nucleons. Misson might be a promis­
ing candidate for "dark matter" which is expected by many astrophysicts to exist in the universe.
 On the other hand, cellron which widely and quickly distributes in the free space like thin films
takes a role of wave like behaviors for the conventional particles. By comparing the two tiny
particles, the dual property of the elementary particles such as electrons and nucleons could be
easily explained.
         For example, let us briefly consider a case of that an electron is put in vacuum and
driven by the electrical potential. The electron could be assumed a combination of a lot of mis­
sons and cellrons. Although there could be a lot of structual strata between an electron or nucleon



                                               - 21 -

                                               -70-
 and the super elementary particles, we now have no information about them at all. The cellrons
expand in vacuum with the velocity of light and fully occupy the vacuum. Here the cellrons
could be confused with "ether," which was very early proposed to explain the propagation of
light. Cellrons are perfectly different concepts from ether, because cellrons are one of constituents
 of matter while ether is independent from matter. The Nattoh Model proposes that the initial
state of the vacuum is assumed to be perfectly empty. Here electomagnetic waves could be ex­
plained as a vibrational motion of expanding cellrons.
         On the other hand, missons are concentrated in a small region of the dimension of the de
 Broglie's length and pulled by the electrical potential. The distribution of missons could be pre­
sented by the density function of the electron on the conventional quantum physics. Since mis­
sons and cellrons are connected each other with an unknown force, the moving missons could
feel some frictional force and not fly beyond the cellrons. This is a physical reason why the elec­
tron increases the effective mass during flying with a high velocity close to that of light.
         During ENC, the nuclei are completely destroyed in which an individual nucleus is also
divided into a lot of missons and cellrons. The missons and cellrons could hardly maintain their
structures during passing through the worm hole. When they are emitted from the white hole,
some mechanisms of their combination could work to form some particles of an upper class.
After such processes are repeated, the conventional particles such as electrons and nucleons could
 be eventually regenerated. Here it would be reasonable to consider that the destroyed materials
quickly rotate in the worm hole and that regenerated elements are separated by centrifugal effects.
 Heavy elements such as Fe and light ones such as C or O could be formed in the central and
peripheral regions of the thin C tubes, respectively. On the other hand, no such effects could not
be expected during the explosion of a black hole so that the distribution of regenerated elements
becomes uniform on the thin C films. The coceptual view of ENG is shown in Fig. 2.
         At the present time, we have no information about how many structural strata exist bet­
ween the super elementary particles and the conventional ones such as electrons and nucleons.
Considering the small dimension for the former, there should be a lot of structural strata. Among



                                               - 22 -

                                               -71-
the strata, we might assume some intermediately tiny particles which have some "genetic" infor­
mation like DNA provided in a bilogical cell. Based on the genetic information, tiny particles of
more upper class could be regenerated.
        When missons and cellrons are separated, some radiations could be emitted. Of course
they should be unknown radiations. A strange radiation was observed with the Egely's wheel
during the continuous USD experiments, as described in Sec. 3.j. It was also detected from the
hands of human beings. This fact suggested that ENC could take place even in a biological org­
anization as will be discussed in Sec. 6.d. and that the unknown radiation could be explained by
the separation of missons and cellrons. On the other hand, no complete recombination of missons
 and cellrons could be expected when they are reborn to the conventional elements during the
eruption of the white hole. Surplus missons or cellrons should be scattered into the space. They
were not experimentally observed as independent particles so far. Some special method should be
 required for the detection of those particles. As will be discussed in Chap. 6, Big Bang could
be assumed as a universal scale of ENC. The discovery of ENG could suggest that a universe
before Big Bang consists of the same elements as that of this universe. Then huge amounts of
missons and cellrons were scattered in this universe as dark matter and free cellrons, respectively.
 Here free cellrons should be similar to ether.
4.h. Electro-Magnetic Effects Caused by /tonic Clusters
         As described in Sec. 3.i, three kinds of ENEM effects were observed during the USD
experiments: the electrical power generation, a new kind of electrical current and the magnetiza­
tion of electrode. They all were caused by the itonic clusters. When the itonic clusters with nega­
tive charges run through a cable wire, it could be a new kind of electrical current. When the iton­
ic clusters, strongly magnetized, are caught in an electrode, it could show the magnetization
effect. Furthermore, when ENC as well as other ENRs takes place in the itonic cluster, it leads to
 the electrical power generation.




                                              - 23 -


                                              -72-
5. Natural Phenomena Caused by ENC

        The preceding chapters described that ENC could be very easily induced in laboratory.

Therefore it is reasonable to consider that ENC could very often occur in a natural environment
as well. This chapter will describe two natural phenomena related to ENC: ball lightning and

earthquakes.

5.a. Natural Ball Lightning(BL)

        Ball lightning(BL) which is one of the most curious natural phenomena was observed

widely over the world so far(33). BL is often associated with thunderstorms which are essentially

 electrical discharges with a large scale. There should be some relations between the natural

phenomena of BL and the USD products observed in laboratory.

        Recently a lot of observation data for BL were collected by several researchers(33, 34)

and international conferences were continuously hold to discuss such issues(35). According to eye

witnesses, natural BL slowly somtimes rapidly flied in the atmosphere and seemed like a shining

and rolling ball or disk. Sometimes it exploded and othertimes disappeared without an explosion.

BL appeared not only on days with thunderstorms but also on cloudy days. It entered a room,

walked around along a wall and eventually disappeared into a stock. Furthermore pilots witnessed

 BL entrering a cockpit, hopping up and down on their handles and disappearing. In summary,

there seemed to be at least two kinds of BL: violent BL associated with an explosion and mild

BL disappearing without an explosion.

        Since BL is natural phenomena and not easy to reproduce, few researchers challenged it

as a serious object. However, the number of the researchers who are much interested seems now

increasing. There were many models proposed for the mechanisms of forming BL so far(35). But

 they all were unsatisfactory for explaining curious behaviors of BL, because they were usually

based on the conventional plasma physics.

5. b. BL Explained as USD Products
        Some of natural BL could be explained by the USD products. Some BL like phenomena

 were observed during the USD experiments, as mentioned in Sec. 3.k. There could be two kinds



                                             - 24 -

                                             -73-
 of BL candidates: itonic clusters and ring products.
         First the itonic cluster is a candidate for the violent BL. It could exist as a stable body
underwater or in the atmosphere. Moreover, the cluster can be driven by the eletrical potential
because of its negative charges and finally explode to disappear. Those characteristics are very
similar to that of the violent BL. Voltex like traces associated with itonic clusters were recorded
on the copper plates during USD. A thin C tube was generated from a tiny white hole in the iton­
ic clusters. In a high itonization, the C tube could not escape from the itonic cluster but roll
around inside the cluster. This is a reason why the voltex traces were left on the copper plates.
Some natural BL with a long thin tail were observed by eyewitnesses. The tail was potentially
made of similar C tubes.
         The second candidate of BL is ring products which were observed during the USD ex­
periments with a thin iron wire electrode, as described in Sec. 3.g. The ring which could also
exist as a stable body showed a very curious property: it decays to a regular hexagonal plate and
at the end of the decay, corona like discharges are emitted from the center of the plate. The ring
might have been generated from an alternative formation of thin C tubes erupted from the white
hole. When the amount of collapsed materials is small, thin C tubes could not be long and have a
 conic shape with a flat top because of a short supply of regenerated materials. Then a hollow
ring could be formed by rolling up the side of the conic tube. Here various materials such as
water which exist near the occurrence point of ENC could be involved in the ring. The ring pro­
duct could be a candidate for the mild BL, because of no explosion. Here such BL should seem a
 disk because the ring is rotating. Among natural BL, there were reports that various kinds of
materials were generated at the break up of BL, for example, a couple of backets of water were
spilled from BL(34). It was very unbelievable but might be explained by the ring products form­
ed from ENC.
5.c. BL Phenomena with Thunderstorms

         When thunderstorms occur, a lot of curious BL phenomena were observed. In 1997, for
example, there was an accident of thunderstorms which fell near the author's house in Sapporo.



                                               - 25 -


                                                -74-
Residents reported many curious phenomena. Visual conditions of TVs were heavily disturbed
during thunderstorms and an electrical switch which was turned off during the thunderstorms
emitted blue light when it was turned on after the thunderstorms(36). The author also witnessed a
 lot of sparks releasing from a transport cable of electricity about 100 m distant from the fall
point. Many of them could have been caused by the itonic clusters. The tiny itonic clusters ge­
nerated by the thunderstorms could run through the cable to arrive at the electrical devices some
what far from the fall point. They could disturb TV, or break up to emit blue light when the
switch was turned on. EDX analysis of the switch is now under way. In laboratory, an accident
of burning a power generator was reported by Chukakov during a similar experiment to repro­
duce the author's USO experiment(37). It was very feasible that itonic clusters generated by the
discharges were kicked back from a cell to the generator through cable wires.
        Mysterious circles also might be BL related phenomena. Fortunately, mysterious circles
were created in Sapporo City in September in 1996 during thunderstorms(38). Then terrible thun­
derstorms occurred three times during three days. The author confirmed the formation of several
mysterious circles each day, which were formed in a small grassland like by pushing down grass­
es with something completely but always forwards anti-clock: about 80 cmcp and 20 cm deep. In
near future, the formation of the mystrerious circles will be made clear by repeating the USO
experiments in laboratory.
5.d ENC Hypothesis for Earthquakes
         Since ENC can be easily induced by discharges, it is reasonable to consider that there
could be other natural phenomena caused by underwater ENC. Earthquakes could be a promising
candidate. During the Hanshin-Awaji earthquakes in 1995, a lot of extraordinary phenomena were
 observed by many residents before lands seriously vibrated: BL like emissions, abnormal behav­
iors of many animals, appearance of seismic clouds and abnormal noises of electromagnetic
waves(39). The phenomena could not be explained by the conventional theory of earthquakes so
that they were completely ignored by the majority of geologists. Among the phenomena, the BL
like emission should be especially remarked. Then the author proposed a hypothetical theory for



                                              - 26 -


                                              -75-
the occurrence of earthquakes as follows,
         (I) There could be underground regions in which strong stresses are concentrated by the
collision of tectonics, as many seismologists predicted. Here underground water which are stored
in strata could be strongly compressed by surrounding rocks. Furthermore weak electrical currents
 which flow underground could continue to slowly progress the itonization processes during a
long period. Then conditions for inducing ENC could be satisfied.
         (2) First ENRs could occur underground sequentially and explosively.
         (3) Explosions could make severe movements of lands which cause damages to human
beings and properties: vibrations, cracks, dislocations and so on.
         (4) Tiny BL like products which are released by ENRs could penetrate through deep
water and rocks to arrive at far places. They could cause extraordinary phenomena in the atmos­
phere as well as underground, as mentioned above. Therefore those extraordinary phenomena
could appear before the lands begin to seriously vibrate.
5.e. ENC During The Matsumae Earthquakes
         In order to verify the hypothetical theory, the measurement with nuclear emulsions
(highly sensitive and especially suitable to detect unknown products) was attempted in the search
for tiny BL like products emitted during earthquakes(14). But it was difficult to select a good
measuring location, because the nuclear emulsions should be set before earthquakes really occur.
Fortunately, there were sequential earthquakes at Matsumae Town (about 100 km west and 180
km south from Sapporo City) on the island of Hokkaido between 1995 and 1996. The nuclear
emulsions were located at Matsumae Town from July 19 to August 18 in 1996. The nuclear
emulsions were same as that used in the previous experiment of cold fusion(40): 100 µm thick

emulsions coated on both sides of an acrylite plate(50 mm x 50 mm x 1 mm thick). 70 % of
them were placed at Matsumae Town and the left over 30 % was placed at Hokkaido University
in Sapporo City for reference, respectively. During the measurement, three earthquakes that could
 be relatively felt by people took place at Matsumae Town, but no earthquakes in Sapporo City.
         Two kinds of extraordinary results were recorded on the nuclear emulsions placed at



                                               - 27 -

                                               -76-
Matsumae Town. Figure 9 in Ref. 14 showed one of extraordinary traces of broken networks
which might be caused by the break up of BL. The trace was extended up to an area of about 5
mm x 5 mm, and such large mesh like traces were never obtained in our laboratory experiments
so far. Remarkably, the network consisted of many clear and straight lines. Figure 9(b) in Ref. 14
 showed an expanded picture for a cross point in the network. Moreover there were another extra
ordinary long tube products, as shown in Fig. 10 in Ref. 14. The length was up to about 3 mm.
One of the products had a conic pole vertically standing at the center of a hole which had a dia­

meter of about 50 µm. A lot of similar conic products were also observed in the previous expe­
riment of cold fusion wth a thin nickel foil(29).
         Comparing the two products with the USD products, the broken network and the long
tube could correspond to the itonic mesh and the thin C tube, respectively. During the earth­
quakes, ENC should be in a great scale. The number of electrons involved in the itonic mesh
should be large and the collapsed materials be much more. Especially interesting, the conic pole
vertically standing in the center hole could be generated from a white hole and the surrounding
tubes could be consistent with the formation of the voltex trases on the copper plates.


6. Discussion
         Here several topics such as cold fusion and other discharge experiments, safety problems
 and impacts to other scientific fields will be discussed.
6.a. Comments to Cold Fusion
         In the previous paper(l), the mechanisms of cold fusion was explained by The Nattoh
Model. Since a lot of valuable information were obtained by the latest USD experiments, we can
comment to the mechanisms of cold fusion.
         The first is the itonic particles(4I). The Nattoh Model introduced new particles to ex­
plain the strong compression of hydrogen clusters as well as aneutron reactions during cold fu­
sion. Then two candidate particles were proposed as called "itons": complex particles consisting
of electrons, positrons and neutrinos, and just interconnected electrons. Some traces suggesting



                                               - 28 -


                                               -77-
the decay of the former particles were found on nuclear emulsions, although the number of the
traces were less than expected(40). As resulted by the latest USO experiments, it was made clear
that most of the itonic particles are interconnected electrons. However, as predicted in the paper,
the followed conclusions would not be so changed.
         The second is chain reactions of hydrogen nuclei. The Nattoh Model proposed two
kinds of chain reactions: itonic interclusters and intraclusters. The time dependent power distribu­
tion was formulated based on the former. The USD experiments now confirmed that hydrogen
clusters really formed the itonic states. But the intercluster chain reactions like neutron chain
reactions in a fission reactior seemed to have been too much emphasized. Rather we have to con­
sider that the chain reactions of D + D --- 'He(with no gamma rays) take place locally and se­
quentially in the itonic clusters. Here the itonic electrons released locally could accelerate more
neighbouring reactions in the itonic cluster. When the electrolysis continues, many itonic clusters
separated in grain boundaries in a palladium metal could be interconnected by overflowing elec­
trical current or expanding the volumes of the individual itonic clusters. Furthermore the itonic
clusters in the metal could be wholy interconnected and reduce the electrical resistence. A group
of McKubre reported the drastic reduction of the electrical resistence of the metal hydroxide
when the excess heat was observed(42).
         The third is nuclear transmutation. A lot of elements were observed during various
kinds of cold fusion experiments so far(28). The Nattoh Model proposed several ways how they
are generated in the itonic hydrogen clusters. The captures of one or more electrons or protons
are the most frequent reactions. Sequential nuclear fusions between the hydrogen nuclei also
should contribute to the nuclear transmutation. Furthermore the fact of that no radiations were
associated with all the reactions could be explained by the compression of the itonic bonding.
However we now have to add another process for the conversion of elements, the electro-nuclear
regeneration(ENG). The elements which were observed in the cold fusion and USD experiments
should be understood as mixtures caused by both of the processes. During ENG, light elements
starting from H could be sequentially generated. Heavy elements were observed up to Fe so far.



                                               - 29 -


                                               -78-
ENG should be definitely distinguished from ENTs as mentioned in Sec. 3.h, because the nuclei
are completely destroyed during ENG. Here no energetic radiations such as gamma rays are
emitted.
           The fourth is the production of tritium. The extraordinary production of T was contro­
vertial in the begining of the cold fusion researches, because cold fusion was misunderstood as
the conventional nuclear fusion reactions between two deuteriums. The Nattoh Model proposed
that the itonic hydrogen clusters could be confused with T. They slowly decay by the break up of
 the itonic electrons and then seem like beta emitters such as T. Now we discovered ENG during
 the USD experiments, which should regenerate much hydrogens as will be discussed in Sec. 6.b.
 It is reasonable to consider that T could be also regenerated during ENG. During the measure-
ment of T, both of the activities from T generated by ENG and the itonic clusters should be
mixed. This problem might be resolved by performing two simultaneous measurements of those
activity and mass.
6. b. Comments to Other Discharge Experiments
           A lot of discharge experiments were carried out by now from early times, especially in
order to make a plasma for inducing nuclear fusion reactions. Although the discharges were
usually made in gas, similar processes to the USD experiment could have taken place. Many
curious phenomena were reported even in early experiments(43). For examle, J. J. Thomson
could never remove hydrogen gas from his chamber after discharges. The curious phenomena
have to be re-examined.
           Discharges in gas or vacuum should be principally same as the USD, except the number
density of involved molecules. Ionized gas molecules created during the discharges could be
driven to an electrode by the potential and become itonized, in which ENRs should be induced.
The nuclear transmutation during discharges in gas was reported by a group of Kucherov(27).
Recently, a group of Pankova reported the BL phenomena during glow discharges and observed
the appearance of hydrocarbon materials(44). Light elements such as hydrogens which were
observed by Thomson could be generated by ENG, although the author have not yet proved this



                                               - 30 -


                                               -79-
due to the trouble of a mass spectroscopy.
         It would be very ironical that during the discharges for making a plasma to induce nu­
clear fusion reactions, the nuclear reactions far beyond what fusion physicists expected already
took place and that the released nuclear energy was used to make the plasma.
6.c. Safety Problems
         Since the whole aspects for the emission of the tiny particles during ENC are not made
clear at the present time, the discussion for safety problems will be limitted to the specified par­
ticles: the itonic clusters and those transformed particles.
         The first is radiation risks for workers who deal with welding. It is reasonable to con­
sider that the electrical welding could induce ENRs similarly to the USD experiments. Here the
current is usually high so that a lot of itonic particles should be emitted. The protection would
not be difficult if they are aware the problem.
         The second is ENG products. It was thought in the previous review paper that materials
broken during ENC should be transformed to unknown and extremely tiny particles, which were
probably hazarderous(l ). However, it is now obvious that the conventional materials are regene­
rated. Since most of them are not radioactive isotopes, ENG might oppositely contribute to the
safety problems. "Material Laundering," which transforms hazarderous materials to safe ones,
might be feasible in near future.
         The third is ENC products during earthquakes. Various materials such as Si are regene­
rated from ENG of the itonic particles which could be emitted during earthquakes. If the itonic
particles are trapped in a human body, they might continue to regenerate materials there. For
example, a renal calculus is sometimes observed in a human body, with those amount could not
be created by body materials. It might be caused by ENG products. This suggestion also has to
wait further investigations.
6. d Impacts to Other Fields
         ENC is a new physics far beyond what many researchers expected. Since the reaction
can be very easily induced anywhere, there are other scientific fields which could be strongly im-



                                                  - 31 -


                                                  -80-
pacted by the discovery. Here we will discuss impacts of ENC to several fields.
        The first is astrophysics. In our universe, the gravitational collapse is one of the most
important reactions, which effects the structure of the universe. Now we have obtained the effec­
tive method for inducing the nuclear collapse so that cosmic phenomena related to the nuclear
collapse would be understood more precisely. Alternatively difficult problems which were never
resolved so far could be well understood. For example, the giant structure of the universe is a
today's most mysterious problem, which cannot be explained by the conventional astrophysical
theory that tiny dusts which were uniformly distributed in the early universe slowly gather by the
 gravitational force and form galaxies. The generation of extremely long cosmic strings is ano­
ther difficlut problem. Furthermore we recently observed curious long tails for the Hale-Bopp
comet, called "sychronized bands." The formation of the bands also seems difficult to explain.
        Most of these difficult problems could be resolved by assuming that Big Bang is a uni­
versal scale of the nulcear collapse. Here the long cosmic strings could have been generated by a
big white hole, like the C tube seen in the USD experiments. On the other hand, spherical films
caused by the explosion of a big black hole could be a good answer to the question of the un­
homogeous structure of the universe. During ENC, a lot of missons could be emitted and scatter­
ed widely in the universe as dark matter. Moreover ENC could make comets which have long
strings generated by a white hole. They might be made of light elements such as hydrogens,
while they emit unexplainable X-rays.
        Here we could propose a very important hypothesis: if Big Bang is a universal scale of
ENC and as resulted by ENG, the conventional elements of this universe were created, a universe
 before Big Bang should be consisted of the same elements as that of this universe. We could
discuss the universe before Big Bang. It might be reasonable to consider that the universe con­
tinues to repeat ENC.
        The second is seismology. There is now a tectronics theory for the mechanisms of earth­
quakes: bombardment of tectronics causes strong stress of rocks which jump back to cause earth­
quakes. This theory completely failed to explain the extraordinary phenomena before earthquakes.



                                              - 32 -


                                              -81-
 Most of seismicists have been ignoring those owen phenomena and spending a lot of research
energy for predicting the occurrence of earthquakes. We now had clear evidence of ENC during
the Matsumae earthquakes. The products caught on nulcear emulsions were very similar, but with
 the large scale due to the natural phenomena, to that obtained in the USD experiments. Of
course, we have to continue the similar observation systematically and repeatedly. We might have
 very certain methods for predicting earthquakes by catching the ENC products.
         The third is geology, which would be effected by the generation of the C tubes and films
 by ENC. Since our EDX was not available to lighter elements than B, hydrogens were not con­
firmed. It is reasonable to consider that hydrogens also should be regenerated by ENC. Recently t
he Russian group of Pankova observed the appearance of hydrocarbon materials during BL expe­
riments with electrical discharges(44), although they had no idea of that the hydrocarbon mater­
ials were creat-ed by either ENT or ENC. On the primitive earth, a large scale of thunderstorms
frequently took place here and there and regenerated a lot of hydrocarbon materials. They were
hurried underground and highly pressurized. This is an alternative explanation for the generation
process of coal and oil. Coal and oil were usually explained as fossiles of plants and animals,
respectively. The theory might be modified in near future. The proof would be made clear by
really producing coal and oil from the hydrocarbon materials produced by ENC. The carbonized
materials were already found during the USO experiments( 17).
         Another comment for geology is volcanos. The eruption of volcanos might be a process
caused by ENC. The eruption could be assumed as an ENC explosion of underground water
which is compressed and itonized by moving magmas. But the author has obtained no evidence
for ENC yet. Lewis predicted that volcanos, tornades, BL and cold fusion are based on his same
principle: common phenomena of "plasmoids"(45). Here it should be emphasized that the plas­
·moid is a different concept from the itonic cluster. The plasmoid could be formed by electro­
hydrodynamic mechanisms, but no nuclear reactions were proposed in the plasmoid. On the other
 hand, the itonic clusters are formed on more microscopic principle of electrons as described in
the preceding chapters.



                                              - 33 -


                                              -82-
        Recently, interesting phenomena were reported, called "Elves" or "Splites"(46). Pilots
witnessed during their flights that fire balls emerged over clouds when thunderstorms took place.
They might be an alternative phenomena of BL, because itonic particles which were generated
during discharges in the clouds could emit upwards as well as downwards. The phenomena might
 be easily simulated in laboratory by making artificial clouds.
         The fourth is quantum mechanics. In 1920s, the quantum mechanics was born to ex­
plain the dual property of an electron, in which the concept of probability was introduced. Then a
 famous controverse was made between N. Bohr and A. Einstein. Although Einstein strongly
opposed against the probability concept, the quantum mechanics succeeded to calculate various
kinds of microscopic problems so that the physical meaning of the duality of an electron have
been left unclear so far. The itonization proccess has remembered us the importance of the pre­
cise and physically clear understanding for the duality of electrons. The Nattoh Model proposed
two super elementary particles of misson and cellron. Particles with the dual property such as
electrons and nucleons are assumed as a complex body of a lot of missons and cellrons. Missons
distributed in cellrons represent a particle like behavior, and cellrons expanded in the space show
a wave like behavior, respectively.
         The fifth is elementary particle physics. The dimension of missions and cellrons are an
               33                                                  11
order of lQ·        cm. On the other hand, that of quarks is 10·        cm. The difference is so large that
there should be a lot of structural strata between them. We have no information about that at the
present time_ Only one suggestion might be ENG, during which the conventional elements such
as C can be regenerated. We might assume that there are some duplication mechanisms during
the formation of elements like DNA working in biological cells. For example, a relatively small
number of missions and cellrons might form an intermediately tiny particles which sustain infor­
mation for restructuring_ Some combinations of those particles might be repeated to form an
upper class of the structure. By repeating such processes, eventually the conventional elements
might be regenerated. It is obvious that ENG could provide a solidified experimental data for
super elementary particle theory. Anyway, elementary particle physics have entered an extremely



                                                  - 34


                                                  -83-
small domain. Since the experiment needs no expensive device, the research would progress ra­
pidly.
         The sixth is biology. This is somewhat speculative at the present time, but might become
 one of the most remarkables in future. There are some data for suggesting the birth of life du­
ring ENC. First some of the C tubes and films which were produced during ENC seem very flex­
ible, and similar to biological tissues. The second is USD products with very curious shapes
which were found on nuclear emulsions in the previous experiment(?). They seemed incompleted
 forms of a biological body. The third is the unknown radiations which were detected by the
Egely's wheel, which is sensitive to the vitality of human being. The fourth is nuclear transmuta­
tions in biological bodies, which were early reported by a group of Komaki(47).
         Now a hypothesis is proposed: ENG could be very closely related to the birth of life.
For example, ENG with a small scale might take place even in biological cells. It might cause the
 nuclear transmutation, emit the unknown radiations, and regenerate the hydrocarbon materials
which are needed to make biological cells. Furthermore, a biological body might be generated by
 ENG with more powerful USD. Anyway the keypoint for the birth of life might be in ENG,
especially in the white hole.

Figure I shows the possible decay schemes for itons i 1 to i4 •
The linear lines could be confused with the background, and                       Fig. I. Possible iton decay schemes.
the double crossing could not be distinguished from two other
overlapping lines due to the focusing resolution of the micro­
scope. However, for more than the triple itons, there are tri­
ple crossings that can easily be distinguished to prove the
existence of the decay of the higher itons.
     Many clear tracks were recorded on film groups 1, 2, and
3. The tracks can be classified into five categories according
to ionization power: (a) star, (b) y-type, (c) I-type, and (d)
rabbit-footprint tracks. Among these, the star, y, and I tracks
are induced by cosmic rays and are also observed on the
background films.
     The rabbit-footprint tracks, which are recorded by par­
ticles with the lowest ionization power such as electrons and
positrons, are widely distributed. There are two types of
rabbit-footprint tracks: a comparatively short and dense track
due to background gamma rays and long, less dense, and fre­
quently crossed tracks due to cold fusion.
     Figure 2 shows a film of the background, in which short
rabbit-footprint tracks left by the background gamma rays
are shown. It also shows a star and an I-type track caused by       Fig. 2. Background (star and I-type tracks can be observed by
                                                                            chance).
cosmic rays, which appear by chance, although they are less
distributed.

                                                               The emission of itons has also been recognized in a sim­
                                                           ilar experiment with ordinary water. 6 Therefore, we have
                                                           proven that a hydrogen-catalyzed fusion reaction, which
                                                           emits no neutrons, actually occurs in cold fusion and that the
                                                           Nattoh model can properly explain the mechanism of cold
                                                           fusion

the hydrogen-catalyzed fusion reaction predicted by the Nat­                                   Excitation (MeV)

     However, some questions remain that should be an­
swered. The first is the difference in the Q values involved.
The modified Q values of 4.77, 3.76, and 2.77 MeV gave                          25.5
good agreement between the calculation and experiment,
while the intrinsic value is 23.85 MeV. We have to explain
                                                                                                ...             23.85
                                                                                                                          d+d
where this difference of -20 MeV in the Q energy goes. The
second is the branching ratio in the two-body fusion reaction.
In the conventional reactions of Eq. (1), the p and n produc­
tion branches are almost equally probable and extremely large
compared with the one for 4 He + 'Y· Furthermore, in the
muon-catalyzed fusion reaction, the conventional reactions
are predominant, as is observed in a high-temperature plasma.                                                  20.6
As the Nattoh model a priori assumes 4 He production with­
out gamma emission [see Eq. (2)], the reason that the 4 He                                                     19.8
production branch exceeds the ones for the n, p, or 'Y produc­                                                            T+p
tion must be explained. The third is the size of the Nattoh,        ltons
which was not discussed earlier in Ref. 2. The way to form
the Nattoh with a given size should also be explained.
     To answer these remaining questions, we will add a new
hypothesis, keeping within the framework of the Nattoh
model. Let us assume that one or more light particles are
emitted during the hydrogen-catalyzed fusion reaction. This
particle then can take away the difference energy of -20 MeV.
We temporarily call it an "iton," which means a "string con­
tacted with the Nattoh." If the iton is a meson, it can be nei­
ther a 1r nor K meson, because the rest masses of these
mesons are -135 and 494 MeV, respectively. Other mesons
have much heavier masses. Thus, the iton could be a new and                                           4He
unknown particle. Another possibility is that the iton could
be a neutrino or electron-positron pair.                                               Fig. 3. Energy level of 4 He.
     The itons should remarkably affect the branching ratio
of the two-body fusion reaction. Figure 3 shows the energy
level of 4 He (Ref. 6). Due to the two-body fusion reaction,

     The last question concerns the size of the Nattoh and the
mechanism for deuteron compression. The deuterons in the
Nattoh must be compressed within the distance covered by
the nuclear force caused by the itons. It is very surprising that
a simple technique such as electrolyzing water has the capa­
bility of compressing deuterons together within such an ex­
tremely short distance. It is natural to look for the reason
within the hydrogen itself rather than searching for an exter­
nal force.
     The hydrogens are assumed to be compressed in two
stages. The first stage is understood from the concept of the
effective mass of an electron. According to the Nattoh model,
electrons connecting the hydrogens move arom'td, following
the third hydrogen. In the process, the effective mass of the
electron increases. Figure 4 illustrates a semiclassical expla­                                    e
nation for the mechanism of deuteron compression.                     Fig. 4. Semicla,�ical explanation of deuteron compression.

     The microscopic structures of palladium metals used for            This technical note describes observations of the micro­
cold fusion experiments are examined. Tiny spot defects sug­        scopic structures of palladium metals that were used for cold
gesting cold fusion have been observed in grain boundaries
as the Nattoh model predicts. The relationship between these
defects and a series of neutron bursts and an indirect loop of
hydrogen chain reactions are discussed.




I. INTRODUCTION

     The Nattoh model 1• predicts that cold fusion 3 . 4 is based
                         2


on hydrogen chain reactions and takes place in the grain
boundaries of the host metal. There are two loops of hydro­
gen chain reactions: direct and indirect. In the indirect loop,
high-energy hydrogens emitted from hydrogen-catalyzed fu­
sion reactions experience slowing down, thermalization, and
diffusion processes in the metal, similar to the loop of neu­
tron chain reactions in thermal fission reactors. The direct
loop, which causes burst emissions of heat and neutrons, is
similar to the loop of neutron chain reactions in fast breeder
reactors, but enhanced in the case of cold fusion because
more short-range particles can be emitted per reaction. If hy­
drogen clusters distribute densely in the grain boundaries, it      Fig. 1. Specimens of palladium rods used for cold fusion experi­
is expected that sequential fusion reactions would damage the               ments.

                                                                 MICRO-EXPLOSIONS
                                                                     Quad-neutrons may decay by the force of gravity like a
                                                                 neutron star. In gravity decay, the nucleus is compressed to
                                                                 a single point and it finally explodes.
                                                                     Figure 5 shows a micro-explosion caused by the gravity
                                                                 decay of a quad-neutron. Two rings, an outer black one and
                                                                 an inner white one, are shown in Fig. 6. The black ring,
                                                                 which was recorded on the surface of the nuclear emulsion,
                                                                 has long flares outside the ring toward the opposite direction
                                                                 of the incident particle. The white ring, which is somewhat
                                                                 transparent, is recorded not only on the surface but totally
                                                                 through the nuclear emulsion. The white ring is separated into
      Fig. 3. Track for decays by Eqs. (4a) and (4b).            four parts, which suggests that the original neutron nucleus

SEARCHING TRACES OF GRAVITY DECAY

                                                                MECHANISMS OF THE FORMATION OF NUCLEI
                                                                WITH MANY NEUTRONS

                                                                     The Nattoh model can predict the production of di­
                                                                neutrons and quad-neutrons by Eqs. (2) and (1), respectively.
                                                                Furthermore, the production of nuclei with many neutrons
                                                                also can be expected by the model.
                                                                     When a hydrogen cluster is formed in a grain boundary,
                                                                it is feasible that a large number of hydrogen atoms, for ex­
                                                                ample, more than ten hydrogen atoms, are contained in the
                                                                cluster. As a result, many-body fusion reactions can take
                                                                place in a metal so that heavy elements such as iron and zinc
                                                                can be produced in grain-shaped defects. 7 During this pro­
                                                                cess, it is reasonable that nuclei with many neutrons can be
                                                                produced simultaneously. One promising mechanism is as
                                                                follows:
                                                                     kD + ke + (N - k)D-+ 2kn + ki 1 + (N - k)d ,
                                                                                                             for k � 3 ,    (3)
                                                                where N is the number of hydrogen atoms in the cluster.
                                                                There could be many variations on the right side of Eq. (3),
                                                                depending on the formation of itons. We can expect the pro­
                                                                duction of multiple-neutron nuclei (6 n and 8 n) fork= 3 and
                                                                4, respectively.
                                                                    On the other hand, nuclei with many neutron!t with a
                                                                mass of >100 could be produced. The production of ruthe­
                                                                nium and indium was verified by the analyses using energy
                                                                dispersive X-ray spectroscopy 7 and radioactive methods. 8
                                                                From the latter _method, 104 Ru and 115 1n were produced.
              Fig. 6f. Traces of the sixth group.               This indicates that nuclear transmutation of palladium takes
                                                                place during cold fusion. The mechanisms of the nuclear
                                                                transmutations can be understood by extending the Nattoh

                                                                   EXPERIMENT WITH THIN PALLADIUM FOIL
     Cold fusion products from the electrolysis of heavy wa­           A cold fusion experiment was performed with heavy wa­
ter have been directly measured by using a thin palladium          ter electrolysis 5• 6 in which a thin foil was used. 7
foil. Several anomalous traces have been clearly recorded on           Hydrogen atoms were charged into a thin palladium foil
nuclear emulsions. Some traces have meshlike structures,           (0.05 mm thick) by electrolyzing heavy water (mixed with 30Jo
which are classified into two types: (a) ones associated with      NaCl). A helical platinum wire was used for the anode. The
ring traces that are caused by the gravity decay of quad­          current was 0.88 A, and the bias was 6.0 V. Figure 1 shows
neutrons and (b) ones with no ring traces. The mechanisms          the arrangement of the thin palladium foil and the nuclear
that form these meshlike traces are discussed in terms of the
Nattoh model. It is inferred that multiple-neutron nuclei such
as quad-neutrons, covered by itonic mesh and iton beads, are
born during cold fusion. Furthermore, other anomalous
traces suggest the production of a new heavy particle during
gravity decay.




INTRODUCTION
     The Nattoh model proposes a hydrogen-catalyzed fusion
reaction as the elementary reaction during cold fusion. 1•2
The reaction involves plural hydrogen atoms and electrons
and thus may have many variations. The primary reaction is
a two-body fusion reaction under many-body interactions,
which can be written as follows 2:
                2D + 2e + D -+ 4 n + i2 + d ,                (I)
where n is a quad-neutron and i2 is a double iton.
        4
    Gravity decay of quad-neutrons was successfully observed
on nuclear emulsions in a previous experiment. 3 The traces
consisted of two spherical rings, an inner white one and an
outer black one. The former could be a broken soup of tiny
materials such as superstrings, and the latter may be the re­
sult of gravitational and antigravitational waves. 4 Measure­
ments were made by setting nuclear emulsions outside an
electrolysis cell so that the quad-neutrons born in a palladium
rod might have been somewhat affected during passage
through the electrolyte solution and the glass cell.
     This technical note describes a direct measurement of cold
fusion products through an experiment with a thin palladium
foil. New anomalous traces have been successfully observed.
Some of them have mesh structures. The mechanisms that
form these anomalous traces are also discussed in terms of the
Nattoh model.                                                       Fig. I. Arrangement of palladium foil and nuclear emulsions.

FUSION TECHNOLOGY            VOL. 23     JAN. 1993                                                                           103

                                                             -154-
Matsumoto       TRACES ON NUCLEAR EMULSIONS




            Fig. 2. Temperature during cold fusion.



emulsions. Thirty nuclear emulsion plates (100 µm thick,
coated on 1- x 25- x 25-mm acrylite plates) were located un­
der the palladium foil, so as to directly measure any fusion
products.
    After hydrogen charging for -4 days, the experimental
setup was cooled in a refrigerator for -19 h to charge more
hydrogen atoms. Ignition of cold fusion was attempted
through the use of a thermal trigger. Figure 2 shows the tem­
perature variation of the thin palladium foil after the cell was
removed from the refrigerator. Although the room temper­
ature is decreasing, the temperature of the palladium foil con­
tinues to slowly increase. It can be inferred that the cold
fusion continued for -3 h.

RESULTS

    The traces recorded on the nuclear emulsions were care­
fully observed by an optical microscope, and as a result, new
anomalous traces were successfully observed.

                                                                      The foil method applied here is very reproducible, unlike
                                                                 the rod method, 5 in which the material is frequently cracked
                                                                 during the hydrogen charging. Sixteen experiments have been
                                                                 carried out so far by the foil method, varying the metal, so­
                                                                 lution, electrolyte, and so on. More than 90% were success­
                                                                 ful in igniting cold fusion. The nuclear emulsion method was
                                                                 also applied to ordinary water, in which similar phenomena,
                                                                 such as gravity decay and the production and evaporation of
                                                                 black holes, were successfully observed. 9 In another exper­
                                                                 iment, a strange picture was taken on the back side of the pal­
                                                                 ladium foil by a scanning electron microscope (SEM), as
                                                                 shown in Fig. 8. The itonic linkage can be clearly seen, as ex­
                                                                 plained in the next section.

                                                                 MECHANISMS FOR THE FORMATION
                                                                 OF ANOMALOUS TRACES

                                                                      Four types of anomalous traces were described earlier. In
                                                                 this section, the mechanisms that form those traces are ex­
                                                                 plained by the Nattoh model.

                                                                 like traces appear to consist of closed links and broken lines.

                                                                 electrolyzing cell.
                                                                      However, the second type of trace, without ring traces,
                                                                 might be produced by other processes. It is feasible that some
                                                                 particle that causes the·second type of trace might be directly
                                                                 produced from the hydrogen-catalyzed fusion reaction, i.e.,
                                                                 an intrinsically vacant mesh. The closed linkage of the vacant
                                                                 mesh might break up because of the instability of the mate­
                                                                 rial itself. Alternatively, nuclei with many neutrons in the
                                                                 mesh cover might produce the second type of trace. They can
                                                                 be produced by many-body fusion reactions of hydrogen at­
                                                                 oms or the hydrogen compression of massive nuclei such as
                                                                 the electrolyte and host metal. When they are transferred to
                                                                 the black holes, 10 no explosive rings are left.
                                                                      Now the questions are what the cover material with the
                                                                 mesh structure is and how it can be produced. The Nattoh
                                                                 model proposes the hydrogen-catalyzed fusion reaction of
                                                                 Eq. (1) as the elementary reaction during cold fusion, occur­
                                                                 ring in a hydrogen cluster trapped in a grain boundary. When
                                                                 the pressure of the hydrogen atoms is beyond some value, the
                                                                 hydrogen cluster can be compressed by a self-compression ef­
                                                                 fect, and then the hydrogen atoms in the cluster come very
                                                                 near each other. The electrons of the hydrogen atoms are also
                     Fig. 6. Curved trace.                       compressed between the hydrogen nuclei. Positive charges are

                                                                   A comment should be added to Eq. (I) of the Nattoh
                                                              model, which produces the quad-neutron. Recently, Miles
                                                              et al. 12 indicated that 4 He rather than 3 He is produced in sig­
                                                              nificant amounts during cold fusion. The 4 He production
                                                              rate seems to nearly correspond to that of heat. This exper­
                                                              imental result seems inconsistent with Eq. (1). However, in
                                                              Fig. 9, the hydrogen atoms that are extremely compressed in
                                                              the cluster are depolarized, as mentioned earlier, but the states
                                                              of the hydrogen atoms are fluctuating quantum mechanically;
                                                              i.e., they become deuterons or di-neutrons. Therefore, some
                                                              variations should be added to the hydrogen-catalyzed fusion
                                                              reaction; for example,

                                                                      Optical Microscope Observations

                                                                          The surface condition of the foils was examined with an
                                                                      optical microscope after the charging. For the potassium car­
                                                                      bonate solution, the front surface of the foils was covered
                                                                      by deposited materials, but the back surfaces were hardly
                                                                      changed. Many circular spots with various colors were ob­
                                                                      served on the deposited layers of the front surface, as shown
                                                                      in Fig. 2. These circular spots were distributed over the front
                                                                      surface and suggested a local and rapid temperature increase.
                                                                      To prove that the circular spots were caused by local discharg­
                                                                      ing, a platinum pin anode was tested; however, there were no
                                                                      similar spots. It is reasonable to consider that these spots
                                                                      might have been produced by the local burning of cold fusion.
                                                                          For reference, a 0.55 mol/t' sodium chloride solution was
                                                                      also tested. Thin hydrided layers were partly formed on the
                                                                      front surface of the nickel. Many colorful rings were recorded
                                                                      on the back surface of the nickel foil that can be classified
                                                                      into the two groups shown in Figs. 3a and 3b. The rings
                                                                      shown in Fig. 3a might be micro-explosions caused by the
                                                                      gravity decay of the neutron nucleus, while the ring shown
                                                                      in Fig. 3b might be an erosion pit. This is discussed later.

                                                                    model. First, the fundamental reactions of cold fusion with
                                                                    ordinary water should be considered. The Nattoh model is
                                                                    based on the hydrogen-catalyzed fusion reaction, which is
                                                                    caused by the self-compression effect of the hydrogen clus­
                                                                    ter. 14 Thus, cold fusion can take place in ordinary water as
                                                                    well as in heavy water. Since the hydrogen atoms and elec­
                                                                    trons involved in the reaction are plural, many variations can
                                                                    occur in the hydrogen-catalyzed fusion reactions. The main
                                                                    reactions for ordinary water can be written as follows:

DISCUSSION                                                         sion. The fundamental reaction during cold fusion is neither
                                                                   the conventional fusion that dominates in high-temperature
    Cold fusion experiments with ordinary water have been          plasma nor the new chemical reaction proposed by Mills et al.5
described. It has been proven that cold fusion really does oc­     It is a new fusion reaction, i.e., the hydrogen-catalyzed fu­
cur with ordinary water to produce excess heat, as previously      sion reaction that was proposed by the Nattoh model.
predicted.4 Measurements with nuclear emulsions have ver­               The difference between nickel and palladium foils is re­
ified that charged particles, such as electrons, protons, and      markable. For the thin palladium foil, hydrogen atoms can
deuterons, and neutron nuclei are produced during cold fu-         easily penetrate through the foil so that cold fusion reactions

FUSION TECHNOLOGY           VOL. 24      NOV. 1993                                                                           303

                                                            -172-
Matsumoto         EXPERIMENTS WITH ORDINARY WATER




Fig. 71. Tiny black and white holes: vertical view, variational sha�.



take place all over the foil including near the back surface.
However, for nickel, with low hydrogen permeability, the hy­
drogen atoms are trapped on the front surface of the foil, so
that cold fusion predominantly takes place on the surface.
Here the reaction products should penetrate through the foil
to be recorded on the nuclear emulsions. In particular, the
itonic nuclei that are heavily charged might be stopped in the
foil. This is why no traces of the breakup of itonic beads and
micro-explosions of the itonic nuclei were observed on the
nickel foil, unlike palladium. On the other hand, hydrogen
frosts were found for the first time during this experiment.
They were not observed in the previous experiment with pal­
ladium. 9 This inconsistency might be caused by thin protec­
tion sheets that were usually located on each nuclear
emulsion. In this experiment, no sheet was placed on the front
surface of the first nuclear emulsion so that the particles could       Figs. 7m, 7n, and 7o. Tiny black and white holes: bottom view.
directly be detected.
     Gravity decays of neutron nuclei and the production of
stars were observed as associated reactions during cold fusion.
It is especially remarkable that many tiny white holes were
discovered. The tiny white hole has spouting and conic zones.           Fig. 7c, and they might be something linked to superstrings.
The nucleons that are absorbed in the black hole are violently          On the other hand, the conic zone of the white hole seems
destroyed so that unknown tiny materials such as superstrings           highly reactive, so that it is recorded on the surface of the
might spout from the white hole. White strings can be seen              emulsion. The tiny black hole might have a reactive filmlike
in the spouting region of the white hole, for example, in               zone around the horizontal zone of the event.

                                                                   Matsumoto      EXPERIMENTS WITH ORDINARY WATER




           Fig. 8. Star (long traces are partly omitted).



    It seems difficult to accept that gravity decay takes place
on the neutron nucleus because the gravitational force is 40
orders of magnitude less than the comparably weak electro­
magnetic force. It should not be understood that the gravi­
tational force predominantly works itself to collapse as soon
as the neutron nucleus is produced. On the birth of the neu­
tron nucleus, the itonic mesh covers it up so that it would not
scatter neutrons, and it compresses the nucleus to destroy its
nuclear structure. As the shrinking of the nucleus progresses,
the gravitational force effectively begins to work on it. Fi­
nally, gravity decay takes place. As micro-explosions of neu­
tron nuclei without an iton were found,11 the compression
process can somewhat be maintained initially after the
breakup of the itonic mesh. Recently, traces of the white holes
have been reproduced in an experiment involving alternating               Figs. 9a and 9b. Scattered itonic hydrogen atoms: frost.
current charging with titanium bolts.17 Tiny sparks have been
observed, and the traces resulting from the sparks are very
similar to those found in the white holes observed in this
experiment.                                                          7. R. BUSH,"A Light Water Excess Heat Reaction Suggests That
                                                                    'Cold Fusion' May Be 'Alkali-Hydrogen Fusion,'" Fusion Technol.,
                                                                    22, 301 (1992).

to wire electrodes of palladium and
